```
  "domain_practice": "How a study of THIS kind (meta-evaluating a reference-free, consensus/sampling-based quality score for structured generation) is built in practice, from what I read and from the run's own record. (1) BASELINES A REVIEWER NAMES FIRST. For any agreement-across-outputs metric, the first missing baseline a reviewer names is SAMPLING SELF-CONSISTENCY from the same model (Wang et al., ICLR 2023). Its semantic version clusters samples by meaning and scores cluster entropy (semantic entropy, Kuhn, Gal & Farquhar, ICLR 2023; Farquhar et al., Nature 2024). For autoformalization specifically, clustering is done by SOLVER-checked equivalence: symbolic-equivalence + semantic-consistency reranking (Li et al., NeurIPS 2024, arXiv 2410.20936) and SMT-based consensus/entropy (Grammars of Formal Uncertainty, NeurIPS 2025). So equivalence classes must be formed with z3, not by string match, and entropy over those classes is a mandatory variant. Round-trip (2604.25031) and an LLM judge are the other required comparisons; they are computed by sibling experiment D on the same item_ids, so this artifact only has to make joining possible. FAIR TUNING of self-consistency: the same model, prompt and format as the candidate generator. Temperature is 0.7 (a 0.5-1.0 range is typical), K is 5-10, and the samples are scored by the SAME equivalence relation as the metric. (2) DATA. The field's NL->FOL sets are FOLIO and MALLS. Per the neurosymbolic handbook's critical rule, never score on them as shipped: 39%/36% of the gold is wrong (2606.02837), so the corrected re-annotations (HF DSAVlab-UNIUD) or doubly-annotated agreed items are the only trusted references. Real system outputs are the credible target (Goyal & Durrett 2021; FRANK). Logic-LM's released FOLIO_dev outputs from 3 systems are the standard public real-output set. ProverQA is template-generated and has no long conditioned sentences (iter-3 probe), so it is not used. (3) CONTROLS. Held constant: the prompt (Logic-LM's FOLIO.txt verbatim), temperature 0, the parser/labeller, and the equivalence relation used by both the metric and the labeller. The confound this design is most likely to be caught on: the metric and the labeller SHARE the vocabulary aligner. A candidate with idiosyncratic predicate names fails to align both to the peers (low eq_frac) and to the reference (non-CORRECT label), which inflates AUROC through a shared instrument artefact rather than faithfulness. The standard fix is to report the score on a subset where that artefact cannot operate. Second confound: pool members that share a prompt or lineage (the 3 Logic-LM systems share few-shot vocabulary) correlate, so a peers-only pool must be reported. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. Item-level binary-label meta-evaluation (TRUE, Honovich et al. 2022) uses ROC AUC with CIs. With about 300 positives and 300 negatives the AUROC SE is about 0.02; with about 100/100 it is about 0.035, so a paired ΔAUROC below about 0.07 is not detectable. Per-stratum cells below 50/50 are reported as descriptive only. Bootstrap: 1,000-2,000 resamples, CLUSTERED by sentence, because the 3 systems' outputs for a sentence are not independent. (5) REPORTING CONVENTIONS. AUROC with a 95% CI; AUPRC together with the prevalence; precision at a pre-registered threshold, re-weighted to fixed prevalences; tie-aware pairwise accuracy for quantized scores (Deutsch, Foster & Freitag, EMNLP 2023 'Ties Matter'), which matters here because eq_frac takes at most 9 values and medoid_depth takes 4; coverage with failures in the denominator; cost in $ and seconds per item; per-error-type sensitivity (FRANK-style); and system-level rank correlation only descriptively (3 systems give no power).",
  "practice_alignment": "MEETS: (a) real outputs from 3 released Logic-LM systems as the primary track, trusted references only (corrected conclusions, or premises where original gold ≡ folio-refined gold); (b) z3-equivalence clustering, not string clustering, plus semantic-entropy over those classes; (c) self-consistency with a matched prompt, T=0.7, K=5, scored by the same equivalence relation; (d) sentence-clustered paired bootstrap on identical items, AUROC+AUPRC+prevalence, tie-aware accuracy for quantized scores; (e) unparseable candidates and peers are counted, never dropped; (f) invariance under STRICT rewrites; (g) pre-registration written and hashed before any AUROC is computed. DEPARTURES: (1) Self-consistency is NOT from the candidate's own model for gpt-4 and text-davinci-003 candidates. text-davinci-003 is retired, and 2023-era gpt-4-0613 via OpenRouter would cost about $30/M input, which breaks the budget. Mitigation: a TRUE same-model SC (openai/gpt-3.5-turbo, the only servable Logic-LM system; note it is the 0125 snapshot, not 2023's 0301/0613) for the gpt-3.5 candidates, plus a cheap-proxy SC on every item. The cost: SC for 2 of 3 systems is a 'single-other-model agreement' baseline, which is weaker than a true same-model SC. This is labelled as such in every table. (2) Few model families and small n. Track L has at most about 202 conclusions × 3 systems plus agreed premises, and the ERROR count is unknown until built (likely 60-200). If there are fewer than 100 ERROR items, the ΔAUROC CIs will be wide (MDE ≈ 0.07-0.10), and the plan says so rather than over-reading them. The released peer-output labels give an extra secondary real-LLM sample (6 families × same sentences) that iteration 2 can pool. (3) Automatic labels only (no panel). This is dictated by the wide-screen design; dataset E adjudicates, and iteration 2 re-ranks. COMPOUND and VOCAB/GRAN-borderline items are excluded from the primary contrast, which biases toward 'clean' errors. Cost: C's strongest signal (depth 3 = far from consensus) may sit exactly in the excluded COMPOUND mass, so a secondary lenient contrast (ERROR ∪ COMPOUND vs CORRECT) is pre-registered. (4) The metric shares the aligner with the labeller. This is closed inside the plan by a vocab-clean-subset AUROC and a partial correlation with alignability, and is reported whichever way it comes out. (5) The long, heavily conditioned stratum is untestable here (FOLIO has about 19 such premises). It is reported descriptively with n, and deferred to held-out dataset E by design. (6) Peer deployment cost: C needs 6 extra translations per sentence. Per-sentence cost is amortised over story-level calls; single-sentence deployment cost is reported separately (MALLS arm) against the $0.002/item gate, with the honest number even if it fails.",
  "implementation_pseudocode": "SETUP (Python 3.12, uv). deps: z3-solver, numpy, pandas, scikit-learn, scipy, aiohttp, loguru, nltk (WordNet for RENAME rewrites; run nltk.download('wordnet') once). Read the skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms and aii-json first. Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, screen.py, peers.py, consensus.py, selfcons.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, cost.py}, data/, results/, logs/.\n\nSTEP 0. Copy and fix the labeller (≈20 min).\n  copy fol.py and repair_census.py from iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/probes/ into src/\n  in fol.py: PREC = {'iff':1,'imp':2,'xor':3,'or':3,'and':4}; fix the docstring\n  tests/test_fol.py:\n    parse('A ⊕ B → C') == ('imp', ('xor', A, B), C)\n    parse('A → B ⊕ C') == ('imp', A, ('xor', B, C))\n    equivalent(parse('∀x (P(x) → Q(x))'), parse('∀y (¬Q(y) → ¬P(y))')) is True\n    for 20 curated pairs from repair_census.rows.json, re-running work() reproduces the recorded cls\n      (differences are allowed ONLY on the 3 ⊕-precedence items; log them)\n  in repair_census.py: change the fol import path only; D = the iter_3 data dir (read-only)\n\nSTEP 1. SHARED SCREEN (identical definition to experiments A/B/D and dataset E; ≈45 min).\n  def norm(t): return re.sub(r'\\s+',' ', re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s]','', t.lower())).strip()\n  def item_id(system, text, fol): return sha1((system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + fol.strip()).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]\n  load the three Logic-LM json files (requests.get with retry; cache in data/logiclm/)\n  for each entry e, system s:\n    parse raw_logic_programs[0] into sections\n    each 'FOL ::: NL' line -> (fol = left of ':::' stripped, nl = right stripped)\n    record the per-sentence premise list and conclusion; keep e['context'] and e['question']\n  curated FOLIO: iterate the jsonl in order\n    story rows: premise NL lines = NL_sentence split on newline; premise FOL = FOL_sentence_old / FOL_sentence split on newline\n      (assert equal counts; otherwise mark story_premise_misaligned and skip its premises)\n    conclusion rows attach to current_story\n  folio_refined_validation.csv: map norm(premise_nl) -> refined premise FOL\n    (split 'nl premises'/'fol premises' on newline; assert equal counts per row)\n  TRUSTED REFERENCES:\n    conclusions: ref = corrected FOL_sentence\n    premises: ref = original FOLIO premise FOL, ONLY if eqmv(original, refined) is True under the SAME labeller (see eqmv below)\n  TRACK L: for each Logic-LM (s, nl, fol) with norm(nl) == norm(curated conclusion NL) -> ref = corrected conclusion\n    elif norm(nl) in the agreed-premise map -> ref = agreed premise\n    report match_rate = matched distinct conclusions / 202\n    MATCH-RATE FALLBACK exactly as in the strategy: if < 150 conclusions match, extend track L ONLY with the agreed premises of matched stories\n    (they are included by default anyway; log that the fallback condition fired)\n    also try matching Logic-LM premise lines via the question/context text when the NL copy after ':::' is paraphrased\n      use norm-equality only; NO fuzzy matching, to keep the screen identical across experiments\n  TRACK H: all 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS)\n    candidate = original gold, ref = corrected gold, system = 'human_original'\n  LABEL(cand, ref, ambiguous) -> one of\n    UNPARSEABLE     (parse fails)\n    CORRECT         (z3-equivalent exactly, OR equivalent after align() where every renamed predicate pair has\n                     identical toks() multisets and every constant pair normalises equal)\n    UNCERTAIN_VOCAB (equivalent only after a rename with non-identical token sets)\n    UNCERTAIN_GRAN  (equivalent only after gran_bridge / reification)\n    ERROR[ops]      (search(base, tgt, budget_s=25) finds depth ≤ 2; ops = sorted list)\n    UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND (no repair)\n    TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN (any z3 call returned None on the decisive step)\n    track H only: if ambiguous == 'True' and not CORRECT -> READING_CHOICE (keep the underlying auto class in a field)\n  STRATA, computed from the REFERENCE formula and the text, so they are identical across systems:\n    words = len(text.split()); len_bin in {'<12','12-19','>=20'}\n    n_quant = number of all/ex nodes\n    depth = max AST nesting depth (every non-atom node adds 1)\n    n_cond = sum over imp nodes of the number of top-level conjuncts in the antecedent\n    exception = regex r'\\b(unless|except|excluding|other than|apart from|but not|without)\\b' on text.lower(); record which marker\n  write results/screen_items.json:\n    [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, label, auto_class, repair_ops,\n      ambiguous, strata{...}, story_id, source}]\n    plus results/screen_label_hash.txt = sha256 of the sorted (item_id, label) lines\n\nSTEP 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any AUROC; write results/prereg.json with a UTC timestamp and the screen_label_hash).\n  Contents:\n    primary score c_score = 1 - eq_frac_full (higher = more likely error)\n    secondary scores: medoid_depth (0-3), cluster_entropy, eq_frac_peers6, sc5_cheap = 1 - sc5_eq_frac_cheap,\n      sc5_same (gpt-3.5 candidates only)\n    operating threshold: flag iff the candidate is NOT eqmv to the medoid (medoid_depth ≥ 1)\n    timeouts (None) count as NOT-equivalent for eq_frac and are logged\n    candidates with fewer than 2 parsed peers get coverage_status = 'insufficient_pool', are scored c_score = 1.0\n      (a coverage failure counts as flagged), and are listed separately\n    primary contrast: track L CORRECT vs ERROR\n    secondary contrasts: lenient (CORRECT vs ERROR ∪ UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND); track H CORRECT vs ERROR\n      (READING_CHOICE excluded and reported separately)\n    bootstrap: 2000 resamples clustered by sentence (norm(text)), seed 0\n    the untestable-stratum rule (<50 errors or <50 correct => descriptive only)\n\nSTEP 3. PEER GENERATION (OpenRouter; ≈1 h wall, async).\n  Read the aii-openrouter-llms skill. Query the live catalog for ids/prices and pick 6 families, one model each,\n  cheapest current instruct endpoint in each family, non-reasoning (disable thinking: reasoning={'enabled': False}\n  or effort 'none' where supported):\n    P1 meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct\n    P2 qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507 (instruct, no thinking)\n    P3 deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3.1 (or v3.2)\n    P4 mistralai/mistral-small-3.2-24b-instruct (or mistral-medium-3.x)\n    P5 google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite (or a newer flash-lite)\n    P6 openai/gpt-4.1-mini\n    If an id is gone, substitute the same family's cheapest successor and log it. Never use an OpenAI GPT-3.5/4 model as a peer,\n    because the family must differ from the candidates: Logic-LM's systems are all OpenAI. P6 is OpenAI; keep it, but tag\n    family='openai', so the peers-only pool can also be reported excluding it ('non-OpenAI peers').\n  UNITS:\n    U_F = 202 curated FOLIO stories+conclusion\n      prompt = FOLIO.txt verbatim; [[PROBLEM]] = story premises NL joined by newlines\n      (use the Logic-LM entry's context when matched, else the curated story NL); [[QUESTION]] = the conclusion NL\n      Premises of a story are translated once per conclusion unit; for premise items use the FIRST unit of that story\n      (deterministic by conclusion id) and log it.\n    U_M = 100 MALLS-test sentences\n      prompt = the same header style with 3 fixed MALLS-TRAIN examples (sha1-first 3 with <=20 words, parseable,\n      exact-gold-looking), each 'FOL ::: NL'; ask for exactly one line 'FOL ::: <sentence>'\n  call params: temperature=0, max_tokens=1500, usage={'include': True}\n    record usage.cost (or compute it from tokens × live price) per call into logs/cost_log.jsonl; keep a running total\n  COST ESTIMATE, BEFORE the sweep, printed and saved:\n    tokens_in ≈ 2.2k (U_F), 0.6k (U_M); tokens_out ≈ 0.7k / 0.08k\n    est = Σ_models 202×(2.2k·p_in + 0.7k·p_out) + 100×(0.6k·p_in + 0.08k·p_out)\n    expected ≈ $1.0-1.5 for all 6 peers. ABORT the sweep if est > $3.\n  concurrency: asyncio + aiohttp, Semaphore(8) per model; 3 retries with exponential backoff; one retry on empty/unparseable output\n    (same prompt, T=0; if still bad, count it as unparseable)\n  parse the response: the lines containing ':::' under 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:'\n    map each to a screen sentence by norm(nl) equality with the story premise NL / conclusion NL\n    if the counts are equal and the text does not match, fall back to POSITION (log mapping_mode)\n    unmapped -> peer_missing\n  save results/peer_outputs.jsonl: {unit_id, model, family, sentence_norm, nl, fol, parse_ok, mapping_mode, cost_usd, latency_s, raw_hash}\n    raw responses go in data/peer_raw/ (small text)\n\nSTEP 4. SELF-CONSISTENCY SAMPLES (≈40 min, async).\n  SC_cheap: model = openai/gpt-4.1-nano (or the cheapest non-reasoning model at ≥ gpt-4.1-nano quality); same prompts as STEP 3; T=0.7, top_p=1\n    5 independent calls per unit (U_F 202 + U_M 100) = 1,510 calls; estimate ≈ $0.5\n  SC_same: model = openai/gpt-3.5-turbo; same Logic-LM prompt; T=0.7; 5 calls per U_F unit with a matched gpt-3.5 candidate\n    ≈ 1,010 calls; estimate ≈ $2.2\n  Run SC_same LAST, and only if the cumulative spend is below $6.\n  Parse and map exactly as in STEP 3 -> results/sc_samples.jsonl\n\nSTEP 5. CONSENSUS SCORES (CPU, ProcessPoolExecutor(4), spawn; ≈1.5 h).\n  def eqmv(a, b):\n    # symmetric equivalence modulo vocabulary; returns (True|False|None, route)\n    if equivalent(a, b, ms=3000) is True: return True, 'exact'\n    for (x, y) in [(a, b), (b, a)]:\n      ax = align(x, y)[0]\n      if fp(ax) == fp(y) and equivalent(ax, y, ms=3000) is True: return True, 'align'\n      gb = gran_bridge(ax, y)\n      if gb and equivalent(gb[0], gb[1], ms=3000) is True: return True, 'gran'\n    return (None if any timeout else False), 'none'\n  cache: key = (sorted pair of formula strings); persist to results/pairwise_eq.jsonl (resumable)\n  for each sentence t: outputs O_t = {Logic-LM s1..s3 (where matched), P1..P6, human_original (track H only)}, parsed ones only\n    compute all pairwise eqmv over O_t (≤ 45 pairs)\n  for each candidate c (track L and track H items; secondary: each peer output as a candidate):\n    pool = O_t \\ {c}; also exclude 'human_original' from track-L pools; the pool is only LLM outputs\n    n_peers_parsed = |pool|; n_peers_total = the number attempted (unparseable peers counted here)\n    eq_frac_full   = mean_{p ∈ pool} [eqmv(c, p)]\n    eq_frac_peers6 = the same over the six fresh peers only\n    eq_frac_nonoai = over non-OpenAI peers only (P1-P5)\n    eq_frac_llm2   = over the other Logic-LM systems only\n    classes = connected components of the eqmv graph on pool ∪ {c}\n    cluster_entropy = -Σ (n_k/N) log(n_k/N)    # semantic entropy with z3 classes\n    medoid m = argmax_{p ∈ pool} #{q ∈ pool : eqmv(p, q)}; ties broken by larger component, then by fixed system order [P1..P6, s1..s3]\n    medoid_support = its partner count / (|pool| - 1)\n    if eqmv(c, m): medoid_depth = 0, medoid_ops = []\n    else:\n      ac = align(c, m)[0]; gb = gran_bridge(ac, m); base, tgt = gb or (ac, m)\n      ops, _ = search(base, tgt, budget_s=10); medoid_depth = len(ops) if ops else 3; medoid_ops = ops or ['COMPOUND']\n    coverage_status in {'ok', 'candidate_unparseable' (score 1.0), 'insufficient_pool'}\n  diagnostics (track L ERROR items):\n    medoid_correct = LABEL(m, ref) ∈ {CORRECT}\n    blind_spot_strict = eqmv(c, m)                # the consensus endorses the error\n    blind_spot_same_ops = (not medoid_correct) and ops(m→ref) == ops(c→ref)\n    majority_endorses = eq_frac_full ≥ 0.5\n    op agreement (depth-1/2 ERROR items, medoid_depth in {1, 2}):\n      exact multiset match of medoid_ops vs labeller ops; any-overlap; coarse-class match\n        (COVERAGE = ADD/DROP; POLARITY = NEG/REV/QUANT; STRUCT = the rest)\n      chance baseline = the majority-class frequency\n  SC scores: sc5_eq_frac_cheap = mean over the 5 samples' translation of t of eqmv(c, sample); missing/unparseable samples count as non-equivalent\n    sc5_eq_frac_same likewise (gpt-3.5 candidates)\n    sc5_entropy = semantic entropy of the 5 samples + c\n  Time guard: log the per-candidate seconds; run mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> all.\n    If the extrapolated total > 2 h, lower search budget_s to 5 for medoid_depth and skip the secondary peer-as-candidate repair search\n    (keep eq_frac for them).\n\nSTEP 6. REWRITE INVARIANCE (≈20 min).\n  select the 150 CORRECT items (both tracks) with the smallest item_id\n  rewrites.py, deterministic, seed 0:\n    RENAME: each predicate's head camelCase token -> the first WordNet synonym lemma from a different word in the first synset\n      (CamelCased); if none, the token reversed + 'Q' suffix (a nonce); constants -> 'c_' + sha1(name)[:4]; arity preserved\n    REORDER: swap the operands of every ∧/∨\n    DEMORGAN: rewrite the first ∧ as ¬(¬A∨¬B) (or ∨ as ¬(¬A∧¬B))\n    PRENEX: pull quantifiers to the front when the variables are distinct and the move is sound (only across ∧/∨, and across the consequent of →); else n/a\n    CONTRAPOSITIVE: every A→B becomes ¬B→¬A\n  assert: equivalent(rewrite, original) is True for all but RENAME; RENAME is equivalent after the inverse map\n  score each rewritten candidate against the UNCHANGED pool with the same code\n  report per family: FA rate = flagged (medoid_depth ≥ 1) among rewritten CORRECT items; flip rate vs the unrewritten version;\n    mean |Δ eq_frac|\n  Expect ≈ 0 for the z3-equivalent families BY CONSTRUCTION (say so). RENAME is the real test of the aligner.\n\nSTEP 7. ANALYSIS (analysis.py; ≈40 min).\n  for each score in [c_score, medoid_depth, cluster_entropy, 1-eq_frac_peers6, 1-eq_frac_nonoai, 1-eq_frac_llm2, sc5_cheap, sc5_same]\n  and each contrast (primary, lenient, track H):\n    AUROC (sklearn), AUPRC with prevalence, 95% CI by sentence-clustered bootstrap (2000)\n    tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq with the optimal tie epsilon; report both epsilon=0 and tie-calibrated)\n    precision/recall/FA at the pre-registered threshold; precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence:\n      prec_π = TPR·π / (TPR·π + FPR·(1-π))\n  paired ΔAUROC with CIs: c_score - sc5_cheap; c_score - sc5_same (gpt-3.5 subset); c_score - cluster_entropy;\n    full pool - peers6 pool\n  cross-fitted logistic on [c_score, sc5_cheap] vs sc5_cheap alone (GroupKFold 5 by sentence) -> ΔAUROC CI\n    Experiment D's disguised judge is NOT available here; iteration 2 joins it by item_id, so also emit oof-ready feature columns.\n  per-system AUROC (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci), each with n\n  per-type sensitivity at threshold: by labeller op class (COVERAGE / POLARITY / STRUCT / mixed) and for UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND\n    with Wilson CIs; the same on track H using the census classes\n  POOL-SIZE CURVE: for k in {1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8}: 20 random pool subsets (seed 0..19), mean AUROC ± sd\n  SHARED-ALIGNER CONFOUND:\n    alignable_frac = the fraction of the candidate's predicates that are identical to, or align() to, a reference predicate\n    AUROC on the VOCAB-CLEAN subset (alignable_frac == 1)\n    partial Spearman of c_score with the label controlling for alignable_frac\n    AUROC of alignable_frac alone (if ≥ c_score's, C is mostly the artefact: report that prominently)\n  BLIND SPOTS: strict and same-ops rates with Wilson CIs, overall, per system, per len_bin, per op class\n    medoid correctness vs the reference by stratum and by op class\n    the ceiling this implies: max recall at medoid threshold = 1 - blind_spot_strict\n  COMPLEXITY: AUROC and FA by len_bin, n_quant (≤1 / 2 / ≥3), depth, n_cond (0 / 1-2 / ≥3), exception y/n,\n    each with (n_err, n_cor) and a 'testable' flag; logistic interaction score×words for c_score vs sc5_cheap, reported descriptively\n  COVERAGE: counts of every coverage_status; peer parse rate per model; mapping_mode counts\n  COST: total $, $ per unit per model, per-sentence deployment cost of C (6 peers) = Σ peer cost / #sentences covered\n    (story-amortised) and the MALLS single-sentence cost; SC costs; seconds per item (z3 + API latency)\n  secondary: peer outputs as candidates (6 families × track-L sentences), labelled by LABEL() against the same refs\n    -> AUROC of c_score over this larger multi-family sample (clearly marked SECONDARY; not part of the shared screen)\n    also the census op-mix of LLM errors per family (feeds C2)\n\nSTEP 8. OUTPUTS.\n  method_out.json in the executor's required schema (validate with aii-json: exp_gen_sol_out), one example per screen item\n    input = text; output = label\n    predict_c_score, predict_medoid_depth, predict_cluster_entropy, predict_sc5_cheap, predict_sc5_same\n    metadata_* = item_id, track, system, strata, eq_frac_full, eq_frac_peers6, eq_frac_nonoai, eq_frac_llm2, medoid_ops,\n      medoid_support, n_peers_parsed, n_peers_total, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds\n    plus top-level metadata with all summary metrics\n  also results/summary.json (every table above), results/screen_items.json, results/prereg.json,\n    results/peer_outputs_labelled.json ({unit, model, family, text, fol, reference_fol, label, repair_ops, strata, item_id\n    computed with system=model}), results/sc_samples.jsonl, results/pairwise_eq.jsonl, logs/cost_log.jsonl\n  make mini/preview variants (aii-json); check sizes with aii-file-size-limit\n  README.md with the headline numbers + .aii/manifest.yaml (all outputs are small text => keep; .venv/ and __pycache__/ => delete regenerable via 'uv sync')",
  "fallback_plan": "F1, Logic-LM files unreachable on GitHub raw: try the GitHub API contents URL, then a git clone --depth 1 of teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM. If all fail, track L falls back to the 6 peers' own outputs as candidates (the secondary sample becomes primary) and this is flagged as a screen deviation in summary.json. The other experiments will have the same problem, so log it clearly. F2, match rate below 150 conclusions: apply the strategy's premise-extension rule only; do not add fuzzy matching. If track L still has fewer than 40 ERROR items, report primary AUROCs as underpowered (show the CI width) and promote the peer-as-candidate sample (6 families, same labeller) to co-primary, labelled so. F3, the premise translations from peers cannot be mapped (norm-equality and position both fail for more than 30% of units): restrict track-L premise items to those with at least 2 mapped peers, and report the loss. Also run a per-premise single-sentence peer prompt (the U_M style) for the unmapped agreed premises, at about $0.3 extra, if budget allows. F4, a peer model fails, is deprecated or returns more than 40% unparseable: substitute the same family's next-cheapest model once. If that also fails, drop the family and continue with 5 (the pool-size curve shows the effect). F5, cost overrun risk: the order of spend is peers (mandatory), then SC_cheap, then SC_same, then the optional peer re-translation of unmapped premises. The running total is checked after every call. At $8.0 all optional arms stop; at $9.5 everything stops, and the partial results are analysed with that stated. F6, z3 blow-up (pairwise eqmv or repair search too slow): lower ms to 1500 and search budget_s to 5. Skip the repair search for the secondary peer-as-candidate sample. If still slow, compute medoid_depth only for track-L and track-H primary candidates and set medoid_depth = NA elsewhere (counted in coverage). F7, the fol.py precedence fix changes more than 3 census classes in the STEP-0 regression: stop and inspect. Only the ⊕ items may change; anything else indicates a copy/import error. F8, eq_frac is degenerate (e.g. more than 80% of candidates have eq_frac = 0 because peers use divergent vocabularies the aligner cannot bridge): report this as a finding, since consensus is capped by alignment. Then run a SECONDARY 'vocabulary-shared' variant: re-prompt the 6 peers with the candidate story's Logic-LM 'Predicates:' block prepended ('use these predicates'), which removes most alignment failures. It costs about $1.2, and runs only if the budget allows and it is clearly labelled as a different metric (it leaks candidate vocabulary but not structure). F9, too few gpt-3.5 candidates match: SC_same is reported on whatever subset exists, with n.",
  "testing_plan": "T0, unit tests before anything else: the fol.py precedence tests; eqmv symmetric on 30 random pairs (eqmv(a,b) == eqmv(b,a)); eqmv(a, RENAME(a)) True for 20 gold formulas whose renames stay token-alignable; eqmv(a, CONTRAPOSITIVE(a)) True; the item_id function reproduces a hand-computed sha1 on 3 examples; the census regression on 20 rows (only ⊕ items may change). T1, screen sanity: print the counts per track × label × system, the match rate, the premise-agreement count and 10 random matched (Logic-LM NL, curated NL) pairs. Assert that no track-L item lacks a reference and that no READING_CHOICE item is counted as ERROR. Save the label hash BEFORE any peer call. T2, prompt dry run: 3 U_F units and 3 U_M units × 6 models (36 calls, about $0.05). Check parse rate, mapping_mode, the cost field being present and the running-total logic. Reject a model whose dry run is 0/3 parseable, and substitute before the sweep. Print the pre-sweep cost estimate and abort if it exceeds $3. T3, mini end-to-end: 20 sentences through STEP 5 with the full pool. Check eq_frac ranges, that the medoid is never the candidate itself (leave-one-out assertion), the per-candidate seconds, and that the entropy is 0 when all outputs are equivalent. Hand-inspect 5 cases where the candidate is non-equivalent to the medoid, and confirm medoid_ops are plausible edits. T4, scale 100 → all with timing extrapolation (aii-long-running-tasks); run in the background by PID and poll the log. T5, confirmation signals before trusting results. (a) Gold-as-candidate sanity: score the corrected reference itself as a pseudo-candidate against the LLM pool. Its eq_frac should on average exceed that of track-L ERROR items; if it does not, the pool or aligner is broken. (b) Planted-error sanity: for 30 CORRECT candidates, apply one typed DROP or NEG edit and verify that c_score rises on at least 70% of them. This is a pipeline check only, never reported as validation. (c) The AUROC of alignable_frac is computed and compared before interpreting c_score. (d) Label vector: the counts in screen_items.json match those logged in T1, and the hash is recorded in prereg.json. T6, final validation: aii-json schema check on method_out.json, file-size check, README and manifest present, and the cost log total equal to the reported spend."
}
```

(1) BASELINES A REVIEWER NAMES FIRST. For any agreement-across-outputs metric, the first missing baseline a reviewer names is SAMPLING SELF-CONSISTENCY from the same model (Wang et al., ICLR 2023). Its semantic version clusters samples by meaning and scores cluster entropy (semantic entropy, Kuhn, Gal & Farquhar, ICLR 2023; Farquhar et al., Nature 2024). For autoformalization specifically, clustering is done by SOLVER-checked equivalence: symbolic-equivalence + semantic-consistency reranking (Li et al., NeurIPS 2024, arXiv 2410.20936) and SMT-based consensus/entropy (Grammars of Formal Uncertainty, NeurIPS 2025). So equivalence classes must be formed with z3, not by string match, and entropy over those classes is a mandatory variant. Round-trip (2604.25031) and an LLM judge are the other required comparisons; they are computed by sibling experiment D on the same item_ids, so this artifact only has to make joining possible. FAIR TUNING of self-consistency: the same model, prompt and format as the candidate generator. Real system outputs are the credible target (Goyal & Durrett 2021; FRANK). Logic-LM's released FOLIO_dev outputs from 3 systems are the standard public real-output set. Held constant: the prompt (Logic-LM's FOLIO.txt verbatim), temperature 0, the parser/labeller, and the equivalence relation used by both the metric and the labeller. The standard fix is to report the score on a subset where that artefact cannot operate. Second confound: pool members that share a prompt or lineage (the 3 Logic-LM systems share few-shot vocabulary) correlate, so a peers-only pool must be reported. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. With about 300 positives and 300 negatives the AUROC SE is about 0.02; with about 100/100 it is about 0.035, so a paired ΔAUROC below about 0.07 is not detectable. ANALYSIS (analysis.py; ≈40 min).\\n  for each score in [c_score, medoid_depth, cluster_entropy, 1-eq_frac_peers6, 1-eq_frac_nonoai, 1-eq_frac_llm2, sc5_cheap, sc5_same]\\n  and each contrast (primary, lenient, track H):\\n    AUROC (sklearn), AUPRC with prevalence, 95% CI by sentence-clustered bootstrap (2000)\\n    tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq with the optimal tie epsilon; report both epsilon=0 and tie-calibrated)\\n    precision/recall/FA at the pre-registered threshold; precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence:\\n      prec_π = TPR·π / (TPR·π + FPR·(1-π))\\n  paired ΔAUROC with CIs: c_score - sc5_cheap; c_score - sc5_same (gpt-3.5 subset); c_score - cluster_entropy;\\n    full pool - peers6 pool\\n  cross-fitted logistic on [c_score, sc5_cheap] vs sc5_cheap alone (GroupKFold 5 by sentence) -> ΔAUROC CI\\n    Experiment D's disguised judge is NOT available here; iteration 2 joins it by item_id, so also emit oof-ready feature columns.\\n  per-system AUROC (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci), each with n\\n  per-type sensitivity at threshold: by labeller op class (COVERAGE / POLARITY / STRUCT / mixed) and for UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND\\n    with Wilson CIs; the same on track H using the census classes\\n  POOL-SIZE CURVE: for k in {1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8}: 20 random pool subsets (seed 0..19), mean AUROC ± sd\\n  SHARED-ALIGNER CONFOUND:\\n    alignable_frac = the fraction of the candidate's predicates that are identical to, or align() to, a reference predicate\\n    AUROC on the VOCAB-CLEAN subset (alignable_frac == 1)\\n    partial Spearman of c_score with the label controlling for alignable_frac\\n    AUROC of alignable_frac alone (if ≥ c_score's, C is mostly the artefact: report that prominently)\\n  BLIND SPOTS: strict and same-ops rates with Wilson CIs, overall, per system, per len_bin, per op class\\n    medoid correctness vs the reference by stratum and by op class\\n    the ceiling this implies: max recall at medoid threshold = 1 - blind_spot_strict\\n  COMPLEXITY: AUROC and FA by len_bin, n_quant (≤1 / 2 / ≥3), depth, n_cond (0 / 1-2 / ≥3), exception y/n,\\n    each with (n_err, n_cor) and a 'testable' flag; logistic interaction score×words for c_score vs sc5_cheap, reported descriptively\\n  COVERAGE: counts of every coverage_status; peer parse rate per model; mapping_mode counts\\n  COST: total $, $ per unit per model, per-sentence deployment cost of C (6 peers) = Σ peer cost / #sentences covered\\n    (story-amortised) and the MALLS single-sentence cost; SC costs; seconds per item (z3 + API latency)\\n  secondary: peer outputs as candidates (6 families × track-L sentences), labelled by LABEL() against the same refs\\n    -> AUROC of c_score over this larger multi-family sample (clearly marked SECONDARY; not part of the shared screen)\\n    also the census op-mix of LLM errors per family (feeds C2)\\n\\nSTEP 8. OUTPUTS.\\n  method_out.json in the executor's required schema (validate with aii-json: exp_gen_sol_out), one example per screen item\\n    input = text; output = label\\n    predict_c_score, predict_medoid_depth, predict_cluster_entropy, predict_sc5_cheap, predict_sc5_same\\n    metadata_* = item_id, track, system, strata, eq_frac_full, eq_frac_peers6, eq_frac_nonoai, eq_frac_llm2, medoid_ops,\\n      medoid_support, n_peers_parsed, n_peers_total, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds\\n    plus top-level metadata with all summary metrics\\n  also results/summary.json (every table above), results/screen_items.json, results/prereg.json,\\n    results/peer_outputs_labelled.json ({unit, model, family, text, fol, reference_fol, label, repair_ops, strata, item_id\\n    computed with system=model}), results/sc_samples.jsonl, results/pairwise_eq.jsonl, logs/cost_log.jsonl\\n  make mini/preview variants (aii-json); check sizes with aii-file-size-limit\\n  README.md with the headline numbers + .aii/manifest.yaml (all outputs are small text => keep; .venv/ and __pycache__/ => delete regenerable via 'uv sync')\",\n  \"fallback_plan\": \"F1, Logic-LM files unreachable on GitHub raw: try the GitHub API contents URL, then a git clone --depth 1 of teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM. No dependency is declared, and the plan is fully runnable from the local files plus the two GitHub raw URLs.\",", "   \"domain_practice\": \"How a study of THIS kind (meta-evaluating a reference-free, consensus/sampling-based quality score for structured generation) is built in practice, from what I read and from the run's own record. (1) BASELINES A REVIEWER NAMES FIRST. For any agreement-across-outputs metric, the first missing baseline a reviewer names is SAMPLING SELF-CONSISTENCY from the same model (Wang et al., ICLR 2023). Its semantic version clusters samples by meaning and scores cluster entropy (semantic entropy, Kuhn, Gal & Farquhar, ICLR 2023; Farquhar et al., Nature 2024). For autoformalization specifically, clustering is done by SOLVER-checked equivalence: symbolic-equivalence + semantic-consistency reranking (Li et al., NeurIPS 2024, arXiv 2410.20936) and SMT-based consensus/entropy (Grammars of Formal Uncertainty, NeurIPS 2025). So equivalence classes must be formed with z3, not by string match, and entropy over those classes is a mandatory variant. Round-trip (2604.25031) and an LLM judge are the other required comparisons; they are computed by sibling experiment D on the same item_ids, so this artifact only has to make joining possible. FAIR TUNING of self-consistency: the same model, prompt and format as the candidate generator. Temperature is 0.7 (a 0.5-1.0 range is typical), K is 5-10, and the samples are scored by the SAME equivalence relation as the metric. (2) DATA. The field's NL->FOL sets are FOLIO and MALLS. Per the neurosymbolic handbook's critical rule, never score on them as shipped: 39%/36% of the gold is wrong (2606.02837), so the corrected re-annotations (HF DSAVlab-UNIUD) or doubly-annotated agreed items are the only trusted references. Real system outputs are the credible target (Goyal & Durrett 2021; FRANK). Logic-LM's released FOLIO_dev outputs from 3 systems are the standard public real-output set. ProverQA is template-generated and has no long conditioned sentences (iter-3 probe), so it is not used. (3) CONTROLS. Held constant: the prompt (Logic-LM's FOLIO.txt verbatim), temperature 0, the parser/labeller, and the equivalence relation used by both the metric and the labeller. The confound this design is most likely to be caught on: the metric and the labeller SHARE the vocabulary aligner. A candidate with idiosyncratic predicate names fails to align both to the peers (low eq_frac) and to the reference (non-CORRECT label), which inflates AUROC through a shared instrument artefact rather than faithfulness. The standard fix is to report the score on a subset where that artefact cannot operate. Second confound: pool members that share a prompt or lineage (the 3 Logic-LM systems share few-shot vocabulary) correlate, so a peers-only pool must be reported. (4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. Item-level binary-label meta-evaluation (TRUE, Honovich et al. 2022) uses ROC AUC with CIs. With about 300 positives and 300 negatives the AUROC SE is about 0.02; with about 100/100 it is about 0.035, so a paired ΔAUROC below about 0.07 is not detectable. Per-stratum cells below 50/50 are reported as descriptive only. Bootstrap: 1,000-2,000 resamples, CLUSTERED by sentence, because the 3 systems' outputs for a sentence are not independent. (5) REPORTING CONVENTIONS. AUROC with a 95% CI; AUPRC together with the prevalence; precision at a pre-registered threshold, re-weighted to fixed prevalences; tie-aware pairwise accuracy for quantized scores (Deutsch, Foster & Freitag, EMNLP 2023 'Ties Matter'), which matters here because eq_frac takes at most 9 values and medoid_depth takes 4; coverage with failures in the denominator; cost in $ and seconds per item; per-error-type sensitivity (FRANK-style); and system-level rank correlation only descriptively (3 systems give no power).\",", "   \"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS: (a) real outputs from 3 released Logic-LM systems as the primary track, trusted references only (corrected conclusions, or premises where original gold ≡ folio-refined gold); (b) z3-equivalence clustering, not string clustering, plus semantic-entropy over those classes; (c) self-consistency with a matched prompt, T=0.7, K=5, scored by the same equivalence relation; (d) sentence-clustered paired bootstrap on identical items, AUROC+AUPRC+prevalence, tie-aware accuracy for quantized scores; (e) unparseable candidates and peers are counted, never dropped; (f) invariance under STRICT rewrites; (g) pre-registration written and hashed before any AUROC is computed. DEPARTURES: (1) Self-consistency is NOT from the candidate's own model for gpt-4 and text-davinci-003 candidates. text-davinci-003 is retired, and 2023-era gpt-4-0613 via OpenRouter would cost about $30/M input, which breaks the budget. Mitigation: a TRUE same-model SC (openai/gpt-3.5-turbo, the only servable Logic-LM system; note it is the 0125 snapshot, not 2023's 0301/0613) for the gpt-3.5 candidates, plus a cheap-proxy SC on every item. The cost: SC for 2 of 3 systems is a 'single-other-model agreement' baseline, which is weaker than a true same-model SC. This is labelled as such in every table. (2) Few model families and small n. Track L has at most about 202 conclusions × 3 systems plus agreed premises, and the ERROR count is unknown until built (likely 60-200). If there are fewer than 100 ERROR items, the ΔAUROC CIs will be wide (MDE ≈ 0.07-0.10), and the plan says so rather than over-reading them. The released peer-output labels give an extra secondary real-LLM sample (6 families × same sentences) that iteration 2 can pool. (3) Automatic labels only (no panel). This is dictated by the wide-screen design; dataset E adjudicates, and iteration 2 re-ranks. COMPOUND and VOCAB/GRAN-borderline items are excluded from the primary contrast, which biases toward 'clean' errors. Cost: C's strongest signal (depth 3 = far from consensus) may sit exactly in the excluded COMPOUND mass, so a secondary lenient contrast (ERROR ∪ COMPOUND vs CORRECT) is pre-registered. (4) The metric shares the aligner with the labeller. This is closed inside the plan by a vocab-clean-subset AUROC and a partial correlation with alignability, and is reported whichever way it comes out. (5) The long, heavily conditioned stratum is untestable here (FOLIO has about 19 such premises). It is reported descriptively with n, and deferred to held-out dataset E by design. (6) Peer deployment cost: C needs 6 extra translations per sentence. Per-sentence cost is amortised over story-level calls; single-sentence deployment cost is reported separately (MALLS arm) against the $0.002/item gate, with the honest number even if it fails.\",", "-  \"implementation_pseudocode\": \"SETUP (Python 3.12, uv). deps: z3-solver, numpy, pandas, scikit-learn, scipy, aiohttp, loguru, nltk (WordNet for RENAME rewrites; run nltk.download('wordnet') once). Read the skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms and aii-json first. Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, screen.py, peers.py, consensus.py, selfcons.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, cost.py}, data/, results/, logs/.\\n\\nSTEP 0. Copy and fix the labeller (≈20 min).\\n  copy fol.py and repair_census.py from iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/probes/ into src/\\n  in fol.py: PREC = {'iff':1,'imp':2,'xor':3,'or':3,'and':4}; fix the docstring\\n  tests/test_fol.py:\\n    parse('A ⊕ B → C') == ('imp', ('xor', A, B), C)\\n    parse('A → B ⊕ C') == ('imp', A, ('xor', B, C))\\n    equivalent(parse('∀x (P(x) → Q(x))'), parse('∀y (¬Q(y) → ¬P(y))')) is True\\n    for 20 curated pairs from repair_census.rows.json, re-running work() reproduces the recorded cls\\n      (differences are allowed ONLY on the 3 ⊕-precedence items; log them)\\n  in repair_census.py: change the fol import path only; D = the iter_3 data dir (read-only)\\n\\nSTEP 1. SHARED SCREEN (identical definition to experiments A/B/D and dataset E; ≈45 min).\\n  def norm(t): return re.sub(r'\\\\s+',' ', re.sub(r'[^\\\\w\\\\s]','', t.lower())).strip()\\n  def item_id(system, text, fol): return sha1((system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + fol.strip()).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]\\n  load the three Logic-LM json files (requests.get with retry; cache in data/logiclm/)\\n  for each entry e, system s:\\n    parse raw_logic_programs[0] into sections\\n    each 'FOL ::: NL' line -> (fol = left of ':::' stripped, nl = right stripped)\\n    record the per-sentence premise list and conclusion; keep e['context'] and e['question']\\n  curated FOLIO: iterate the jsonl in order\\n    story rows: premise NL lines = NL_sentence split on newline; premise FOL = FOL_sentence_old / FOL_sentence split on newline\\n      (assert equal counts; otherwise mark story_premise_misaligned and skip its premises)\\n    conclusion rows attach to current_story\\n  folio_refined_validation.csv: map norm(premise_nl) -> refined premise FOL\\n    (split 'nl premises'/'fol premises' on newline; assert equal counts per row)\\n  TRUSTED REFERENCES:\\n    conclusions: ref = corrected FOL_sentence\\n    premises: ref = original FOLIO premise FOL, ONLY if eqmv(original, refined) is True under the SAME labeller (see eqmv below)\\n  TRACK L: for each Logic-LM (s, nl, fol) with norm(nl) == norm(curated conclusion NL) -> ref = corrected conclusion\\n    elif norm(nl) in the agreed-premise map -> ref = agreed premise\\n    report match_rate = matched distinct conclusions / 202\\n    MATCH-RATE FALLBACK exactly as in the strategy: if < 150 conclusions match, extend track L ONLY with the agreed premises of matched stories\\n    (they are included by default anyway; log that the fallback condition fired)\\n    also try matching Logic-LM premise lines via the question/context text when the NL copy after ':::' is paraphrased\\n      use norm-equality only; NO fuzzy matching, to keep the screen identical across experiments\\n  TRACK H: all 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS)\\n    candidate = original gold, ref = corrected gold, system = 'human_original'\\n  LABEL(cand, ref, ambiguous) -> one of\\n    UNPARSEABLE     (parse fails)\\n    CORRECT         (z3-equivalent exactly, OR equivalent after align() where every renamed predicate pair has\\n                     identical toks() multisets and every constant pair normalises equal)\\n    UNCERTAIN_VOCAB (equivalent only after a rename with non-identical token sets)\\n    UNCERTAIN_GRAN  (equivalent only after gran_bridge / reification)\\n    ERROR[ops]      (search(base, tgt, budget_s=25) finds depth ≤ 2; ops = sorted list)\\n    UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND (no repair)\\n    TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN (any z3 call returned None on the decisive step)\\n    track H only: if ambiguous == 'True' and not CORRECT -> READING_CHOICE (keep the underlying auto class in a field)\\n  STRATA, computed from the REFERENCE formula and the text, so they are identical across systems:\\n    words = len(text.split()); len_bin in {'<12','12-19','>=20'}\\n    n_quant = number of all/ex nodes\\n    depth = max AST nesting depth (every non-atom node adds 1)\\n    n_cond = sum over imp nodes of the number of top-level conjuncts in the antecedent\\n    exception = regex r'\\\\b(unless|except|excluding|other than|apart from|but not|without)\\\\b' on text.lower(); record which marker\\n  write results/screen_items.json:\\n    [{item_id, track, system, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, label, auto_class, repair_ops,\\n      ambiguous, strata{...}, story_id, source}]\\n    plus results/screen_label_hash.txt = sha256 of the sorted (item_id, label) lines\\n\\nSTEP 2. PRE-REGISTRATION (before any AUROC; write results/prereg.json with a UTC timestamp and the screen_label_hash).\\n  Contents:\\n    primary score c_score = 1 - eq_frac_full (higher = more likely error)\\n    secondary scores: medoid_depth (0-3), cluster_entropy, eq_frac_peers6, sc5_cheap = 1 - sc5_eq_frac_cheap,\\n      sc5_same (gpt-3.5 candidates only)\\n    operating threshold: flag iff the candidate is NOT eqmv to the medoid (medoid_depth ≥ 1)\\n    timeouts (None) count as NOT-equivalent for eq_frac and are logged\\n    candidates with fewer than 2 parsed peers get coverage_status = 'insufficient_pool', are scored c_score = 1.0\\n      (a coverage failure counts as flagged), and are listed separately\\n    primary contrast: track L CORRECT vs ERROR\\n    secondary contrasts: lenient (CORRECT vs ERROR ∪ UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND); track H CORRECT vs ERROR\\n      (READING_CHOICE excluded and reported separately)\\n    bootstrap: 2000 resamples clustered by sentence (norm(text)), seed 0\\n    the untestable-stratum rule (<50 errors or <50 correct => descriptive only)\\n\\nSTEP 3. PEER GENERATION (OpenRouter; ≈1 h wall, async).\\n  Read the aii-openrouter-llms skill. Query the live catalog for ids/prices and pick 6 families, one model each,\\n  cheapest current instruct endpoint in each family, non-reasoning (disable thinking: reasoning={'enabled': False}\\n  or effort 'none' where supported):\\n    P1 meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct\\n    P2 qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507 (instruct, no thinking)\\n    P3 deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3.1 (or v3.2)\\n    P4 mistralai/mistral-small-3.2-24b-instruct (or mistral-medium-3.x)\\n    P5 google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite (or a newer flash-lite)\\n    P6 openai/gpt-4.1-mini\\n    If an id is gone, substitute the same family's cheapest successor and log it. Never use an OpenAI GPT-3.5/4 model as a peer,\\n    because the family must differ from the candidates: Logic-LM's systems are all OpenAI. P6 is OpenAI; keep it, but tag\\n    family='openai', so the peers-only pool can also be reported excluding it ('non-OpenAI peers').\\n  UNITS:\\n    U_F = 202 curated FOLIO stories+conclusion\\n      prompt = FOLIO.txt verbatim; [[PROBLEM]] = story premises NL joined by newlines\\n      (use the Logic-LM entry's context when matched, else the curated story NL); [[QUESTION]] = the conclusion NL\\n      Premises of a story are translated once per conclusion unit; for premise items use the FIRST unit of that story\\n      (deterministic by conclusion id) and log it.\\n    U_M = 100 MALLS-test sentences\\n      prompt = the same header style with 3 fixed MALLS-TRAIN examples (sha1-first 3 with <=20 words, parseable,\\n      exact-gold-looking), each 'FOL ::: NL'; ask for exactly one line 'FOL ::: <sentence>'\\n  call params: temperature=0, max_tokens=1500, usage={'include': True}\\n    record usage.cost (or compute it from tokens × live price) per call into logs/cost_log.jsonl; keep a running total\\n  COST ESTIMATE, BEFORE the sweep, printed and saved:\\n    tokens_in ≈ 2.2k (U_F), 0.6k (U_M); tokens_out ≈ 0.7k / 0.08k\\n    est = Σ_models 202×(2.2k·p_in + 0.7k·p_out) + 100×(0.6k·p_in + 0.08k·p_out)\\n    expected ≈ $1.0-1.5 for all 6 peers. ABORT the sweep if est > $3.\\n  concurrency: asyncio + aiohttp, Semaphore(8) per model; 3 retries with exponential backoff; one retry on empty/unparseable output\\n    (same prompt, T=0; if still bad, count it as unparseable)\\n  parse the response: the lines containing ':::' under 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:'\\n    map each to a screen sentence by norm(nl) equality with the story premise NL / conclusion NL\\n    if the counts are equal and the text does not match, fall back to POSITION (log mapping_mode)\\n    unmapped -> peer_missing\\n  save results/peer_outputs.jsonl: {unit_id, model, family, sentence_norm, nl, fol, parse_ok, mapping_mode, cost_usd, latency_s, raw_hash}\\n    raw responses go in data/peer_raw/ (small text)\\n\\nSTEP 4. SELF-CONSISTENCY SAMPLES (≈40 min, async).\\n  SC_cheap: model = openai/gpt-4.1-nano (or the cheapest non-reasoning model at ≥ gpt-4.1-nano quality); same prompts as STEP 3; T=0.7, top_p=1\\n    5 independent calls per unit (U_F 202 + U_M 100) = 1,510 calls; estimate ≈ $0.5\\n  SC_same: model = openai/gpt-3.5-turbo; same Logic-LM prompt; T=0.7; 5 calls per U_F unit with a matched gpt-3.5 candidate\\n    ≈ 1,010 calls; estimate ≈ $2.2\\n  Run SC_same LAST, and only if the cumulative spend is below $6.\\n  Parse and map exactly as in STEP 3 -> results/sc_samples.jsonl\\n\\nSTEP 5. CONSENSUS SCORES (CPU, ProcessPoolExecutor(4), spawn; ≈1.5 h).\\n  def eqmv(a, b):\\n    # symmetric equivalence modulo vocabulary; returns (True|False|None, route)\\n    if equivalent(a, b, ms=3000) is True: return True, 'exact'\\n    for (x, y) in [(a, b), (b, a)]:\\n      ax = align(x, y)[0]\\n      if fp(ax) == fp(y) and equivalent(ax, y, ms=3000) is True: return True, 'align'\\n      gb = gran_bridge(ax, y)\\n      if gb and equivalent(gb[0], gb[1], ms=3000) is True: return True, 'gran'\\n    return (None if any timeout else False), 'none'\\n  cache: key = (sorted pair of formula strings); persist to results/pairwise_eq.jsonl (resumable)\\n  for each sentence t: outputs O_t = {Logic-LM s1..s3 (where matched), P1..P6, human_original (track H only)}, parsed ones only\\n    compute all pairwise eqmv over O_t (≤ 45 pairs)\\n  for each candidate c (track L and track H items; secondary: each peer output as a candidate):\\n    pool = O_t \\\\ {c}; also exclude 'human_original' from track-L pools; the pool is only LLM outputs\\n    n_peers_parsed = |pool|; n_peers_total = the number attempted (unparseable peers counted here)\\n    eq_frac_full   = mean_{p ∈ pool} [eqmv(c, p)]\\n    eq_frac_peers6 = the same over the six fresh peers only\\n    eq_frac_nonoai = over non-OpenAI peers only (P1-P5)\\n    eq_frac_llm2   = over the other Logic-LM systems only\\n    classes = connected components of the eqmv graph on pool ∪ {c}\\n    cluster_entropy = -Σ (n_k/N) log(n_k/N)    # semantic entropy with z3 classes\\n    medoid m = argmax_{p ∈ pool} #{q ∈ pool : eqmv(p, q)}; ties broken by larger component, then by fixed system order [P1..P6, s1..s3]\\n    medoid_support = its partner count / (|pool| - 1)\\n    if eqmv(c, m): medoid_depth = 0, medoid_ops = []\\n    else:\\n      ac = align(c, m)[0]; gb = gran_bridge(ac, m); base, tgt = gb or (ac, m)\\n      ops, _ = search(base, tgt, budget_s=10); medoid_depth = len(ops) if ops else 3; medoid_ops = ops or ['COMPOUND']\\n    coverage_status in {'ok', 'candidate_unparseable' (score 1.0), 'insufficient_pool'}\\n  diagnostics (track L ERROR items):\\n    medoid_correct = LABEL(m, ref) ∈ {CORRECT}\\n    blind_spot_strict = eqmv(c, m)                # the consensus endorses the error\\n    blind_spot_same_ops = (not medoid_correct) and ops(m→ref) == ops(c→ref)\\n    majority_endorses = eq_frac_full ≥ 0.5\\n    op agreement (depth-1/2 ERROR items, medoid_depth in {1, 2}):\\n      exact multiset match of medoid_ops vs labeller ops; any-overlap; coarse-class match\\n        (COVERAGE = ADD/DROP; POLARITY = NEG/REV/QUANT; STRUCT = the rest)\\n      chance baseline = the majority-class frequency\\n  SC scores: sc5_eq_frac_cheap = mean over the 5 samples' translation of t of eqmv(c, sample); missing/unparseable samples count as non-equivalent\\n    sc5_eq_frac_same likewise (gpt-3.5 candidates)\\n    sc5_entropy = semantic entropy of the 5 samples + c\\n  Time guard: log the per-candidate seconds; run mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> all.\\n    If the extrapolated total > 2 h, lower search budget_s to 5 for medoid_depth and skip the secondary peer-as-candidate repair search\\n    (keep eq_frac for them).\\n\\nSTEP 6. REWRITE INVARIANCE (≈20 min).\\n  select the 150 CORRECT items (both tracks) with the smallest item_id\\n  rewrites.py, deterministic, seed 0:\\n    RENAME: each predicate's head camelCase token -> the first WordNet synonym lemma from a different word in the first synset\\n      (CamelCased); if none, the token reversed + 'Q' suffix (a nonce); constants -> 'c_' + sha1(name)[:4]; arity preserved\\n    REORDER: swap the operands of every ∧/∨\\n    DEMORGAN: rewrite the first ∧ as ¬(¬A∨¬B) (or ∨ as ¬(¬A∧¬B))\\n    PRENEX: pull quantifiers to the front when the variables are distinct and the move is sound (only across ∧/∨, and across the consequent of →); else n/a\\n    CONTRAPOSITIVE: every A→B becomes ¬B→¬A\\n  assert: equivalent(rewrite, original) is True for all but RENAME; RENAME is equivalent after the inverse map\\n  score each rewritten candidate against the UNCHANGED pool with the same code\\n  report per family: FA rate = flagged (medoid_depth ≥ 1) among rewritten CORRECT items; flip rate vs the unrewritten version;\\n    mean |Δ eq_frac|\\n  Expect ≈ 0 for the z3-equivalent families BY CONSTRUCTION (say so). RENAME is the real test of the aligner.\\n\\nSTEP 7. ANALYSIS (analysis.py; ≈40 min).\\n  for each score in [c_score, medoid_depth, cluster_entropy, 1-eq_frac_peers6, 1-eq_frac_nonoai, 1-eq_frac_llm2, sc5_cheap, sc5_same]\\n  and each contrast (primary, lenient, track H):\\n    AUROC (sklearn), AUPRC with prevalence, 95% CI by sentence-clustered bootstrap (2000)\\n    tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq with the optimal tie epsilon; report both epsilon=0 and tie-calibrated)\\n    precision/recall/FA at the pre-registered threshold; precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence:\\n      prec_π = TPR·π / (TPR·π + FPR·(1-π))\\n  paired ΔAUROC with CIs: c_score - sc5_cheap; c_score - sc5_same (gpt-3.5 subset); c_score - cluster_entropy;\\n    full pool - peers6 pool\\n  cross-fitted logistic on [c_score, sc5_cheap] vs sc5_cheap alone (GroupKFold 5 by sentence) -> ΔAUROC CI\\n    Experiment D's disguised judge is NOT available here; iteration 2 joins it by item_id, so also emit oof-ready feature columns.\\n  per-system AUROC (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci), each with n\\n  per-type sensitivity at threshold: by labeller op class (COVERAGE / POLARITY / STRUCT / mixed) and for UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND\\n    with Wilson CIs; the same on track H using the census classes\\n  POOL-SIZE CURVE: for k in {1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8}: 20 random pool subsets (seed 0..19), mean AUROC ± sd\\n  SHARED-ALIGNER CONFOUND:\\n    alignable_frac = the fraction of the candidate's predicates that are identical to, or align() to, a reference predicate\\n    AUROC on the VOCAB-CLEAN subset (alignable_frac == 1)\\n    partial Spearman of c_score with the label controlling for alignable_frac\\n    AUROC of alignable_frac alone (if ≥ c_score's, C is mostly the artefact: report that prominently)\\n  BLIND SPOTS: strict and same-ops rates with Wilson CIs, overall, per system, per len_bin, per op class\\n    medoid correctness vs the reference by stratum and by op class\\n    the ceiling this implies: max recall at medoid threshold = 1 - blind_spot_strict\\n  COMPLEXITY: AUROC and FA by len_bin, n_quant (≤1 / 2 / ≥3), depth, n_cond (0 / 1-2 / ≥3), exception y/n,\\n    each with (n_err, n_cor) and a 'testable' flag; logistic interaction score×words for c_score vs sc5_cheap, reported descriptively\\n  COVERAGE: counts of every coverage_status; peer parse rate per model; mapping_mode counts\\n  COST: total $, $ per unit per model, per-sentence deployment cost of C (6 peers) = Σ peer cost / #sentences covered\\n    (story-amortised) and the MALLS single-sentence cost; SC costs; seconds per item (z3 + API latency)\\n  secondary: peer outputs as candidates (6 families × track-L sentences), labelled by LABEL() against the same refs\\n    -> AUROC of c_score over this larger multi-family sample (clearly marked SECONDARY; not part of the shared screen)\\n    also the census op-mix of LLM errors per family (feeds C2)\\n\\nSTEP 8. OUTPUTS.\\n  method_out.json in the executor's required schema (validate with aii-json: exp_gen_sol_out), one example per screen item\\n    input = text; output = label\\n    predict_c_score, predict_medoid_depth, predict_cluster_entropy, predict_sc5_cheap, predict_sc5_same\\n    metadata_* = item_id, track, system, strata, eq_frac_full, eq_frac_peers6, eq_frac_nonoai, eq_frac_llm2, medoid_ops,\\n      medoid_support, n_peers_parsed, n_peers_total, coverage_status, cost_usd, seconds\\n    plus top-level metadata with all summary metrics\\n  also results/summary.json (every table above), results/screen_items.json, results/prereg.json,\\n    results/peer_outputs_labelled.json ({unit, model, family, text, fol, reference_fol, label, repair_ops, strata, item_id\\n    computed with system=model}), results/sc_samples.jsonl, results/pairwise_eq.jsonl, logs/cost_log.jsonl\\n  make mini/preview variants (aii-json); check sizes with aii-file-size-limit\\n  README.md with the headline numbers + .aii/manifest.yaml (all outputs are small text => keep; .venv/ and __pycache__/ => delete regenerable via 'uv sync')\",", "+  \"implementation_pseudocode\": \"SETUP (Python 3.12, uv). deps: z3-solver, numpy, pandas, scikit-learn, scipy, aiohttp, loguru, nltk (WordNet for RENAME rewrites; run nltk.download('wordnet') once). Read the skills aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms and aii-json first. Layout: src/{fol.py, repair_census.py, screen.py, peers.py, consensus.py, selfcons.py, rewrites.py, analysis.py, cost.py}, data/, results/, logs/.\\n\\nSTEP 0. log that the fallback condition fired)\\n    also try matching Logic-LM premise lines via the question/context text when the NL copy after ':::' is paraphrased\\n      use norm-equality only; NO fuzzy matching, to keep the screen identical across experiments\\n  TRACK H: all 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS)\\n    candidate = original gold, ref = corrected gold, system = 'human_original'\\n  LABEL(cand, ref, ambiguous) -> one of\\n    UNPARSEABLE     (parse fails)\\n    CORRECT         (z3-equivalent exactly [auto_class EQUIV], OR equivalent after align() [auto_class VOCAB])\\n                     This matches the sibling Exp-A plan (EQUIV+VOCAB -> CORRECT, GRAN+COMPOUND -> UNCERTAIN), so label vectors join byte-identically.\\n                     Also emit the flag vocab_strict = every renamed predicate pair has identical toks() multisets;\\n                     a secondary analysis treats non-strict VOCAB as uncertain.\\n    UNCERTAIN_GRAN  (equivalent only after gran_bridge / reification)\\n    ERROR[ops]      (search(base, tgt, budget_s=25) finds depth ≤ 2; Query the live catalog for ids/prices and pick 6 families, one model each,\\n  cheapest current instruct endpoint in each family, non-reasoning (disable thinking: reasoning={'enabled': False}\\n  or effort 'none' where supported):\\n    P1 meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct\\n    P2 qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507 (instruct, no thinking)\\n    P3 deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3.1 (or v3.2)\\n    P4 mistralai/mistral-small-3.2-24b-instruct (or mistral-medium-3.x)\\n    P5 google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite (or a newer flash-lite)\\n    P6 openai/gpt-4.1-mini\\n    If an id is gone, substitute the same family's cheapest successor and log it. Never use an OpenAI GPT-3.5/4 model as a peer,\\n    because the family must differ from the candidates: Logic-LM's systems are all OpenAI. P6 is OpenAI; keep it, but tag\\n    family='openai', so the peers-only pool can also be reported excluding it ('non-OpenAI peers').\\n  UNITS:\\n    U_F = 202 curated FOLIO stories+conclusion\\n      prompt = FOLIO.txt verbatim; [[PROBLEM]] = story premises NL joined by newlines\\n      (use the Logic-LM entry's context when matched, else the curated story NL); [[QUESTION]] = the conclusion NL\\n      Premises of a story are translated once per conclusion unit; for premise items use the FIRST unit of that story\\n      (deterministic by conclusion id) and log it.\\n    U_M = 100 MALLS-test sentences\\n      prompt = the same header style with 3 fixed MALLS-TRAIN examples (sha1-first 3 with <=20 words, parseable,\\n      exact-gold-looking), each 'FOL ::: NL'; ask for exactly one line 'FOL ::: <sentence>'\\n  call params: temperature=0, max_tokens=1500, usage={'include': True}\\n    record usage.cost (or compute it from tokens × live price) per call into logs/cost_log.jsonl; keep a running total\\n  COST ESTIMATE, BEFORE the sweep, printed and saved:\\n    tokens_in ≈ 2.2k (U_F), 0.6k (U_M); tokens_out ≈ 0.7k / 0.08k\\n    est = Σ_models 202×(2.2k·p_in + 0.7k·p_out) + 100×(0.6k·p_in + 0.08k·p_out)\\n    expected ≈ $1.0-1.5 for all 6 peers. ABORT the sweep if est > $3.\\n  concurrency: asyncio + aiohttp, Semaphore(8) per model; 3 retries with exponential backoff; one retry on empty/unparseable output\\n    (same prompt, T=0; if still bad, count it as unparseable)\\n  parse the response: the lines containing ':::' under 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:'\\n    map each to a screen sentence by norm(nl) equality with the story premise NL / conclusion NL\\n    if the counts are equal and the text does not match, fall back to POSITION (log mapping_mode)\\n    unmapped -> peer_missing\\n  save results/peer_outputs.jsonl: {unit_id, model, family, sentence_norm, nl, fol, parse_ok, mapping_mode, cost_usd, latency_s, raw_hash}\\n    raw responses go in data/peer_raw/ (small text)\\n\\nSTEP 4. SELF-CONSISTENCY SAMPLES (≈40 min, async).\\n  SC_cheap: model = openai/gpt-4.1-nano (or the cheapest non-reasoning model at ≥ gpt-4.1-nano quality); same prompts as STEP 3; T=0.7, top_p=1\\n    5 independent calls per unit (U_F 202 + U_M 100) = 1,510 calls; estimate ≈ $0.5\\n  SC_same: model = openai/gpt-3.5-turbo; same Logic-LM prompt; T=0.7; 5 calls per U_F unit with a matched gpt-3.5 candidate\\n    ≈ 1,010 calls; estimate ≈ $2.2\\n  Run SC_same LAST, and only if the cumulative spend is below $6.\\n  Parse and map exactly as in STEP 3 -> results/sc_samples.jsonl\\n\\nSTEP 5. CONSENSUS SCORES (CPU, ProcessPoolExecutor(4), spawn; ≈1.5 h).\\n  def eqmv(a, b):\\n    # symmetric equivalence modulo vocabulary; returns (True|False|None, route)\\n    if equivalent(a, b, ms=3000) is True: return True, 'exact'\\n    for (x, y) in [(a, b), (b, a)]:\\n      ax = align(x, y)[0]\\n      if fp(ax) == fp(y) and equivalent(ax, y, ms=3000) is True: return True, 'align'\\n      gb = gran_bridge(ax, y)\\n      if gb and equivalent(gb[0], gb[1], ms=3000) is True: return True, 'gran'\\n    return (None if any timeout else False), 'none'\\n  cache: key = (sorted pair of formula strings); persist to results/pairwise_eq.jsonl (resumable)\\n  for each sentence t: outputs O_t = {Logic-LM s1..s3 (where matched), P1..P6, human_original (track H only)}, parsed ones only\\n    compute all pairwise eqmv over O_t (≤ 45 pairs)\\n  for each candidate c (track L and track H items; secondary: each peer output as a candidate):\\n    pool = O_t \\\\ {c}; also exclude 'human_original' from track-L pools; the pool is only LLM outputs\\n    n_peers_parsed = |pool|; n_peers_total = the number attempted (unparseable peers counted here)\\n    eq_frac_full   = mean_{p ∈ pool} [eqmv(c, p)]\\n    eq_frac_peers6 = the same over the six fresh peers only\\n    eq_frac_nonoai = over non-OpenAI peers only (P1-P5)\\n    eq_frac_llm2   = over the other Logic-LM systems only\\n    classes = connected components of the eqmv graph on pool ∪ {c}\\n    cluster_entropy = -Σ (n_k/N) log(n_k/N)    # semantic entropy with z3 classes\\n    medoid m = argmax_{p ∈ pool} #{q ∈ pool : eqmv(p, q)}; ties broken by larger component, then by fixed system order [P1..P6, s1..s3]\\n    medoid_support = its partner count / (|pool| - 1)\\n    if eqmv(c, m): medoid_depth = 0, medoid_ops = []\\n    else:\\n      ac = align(c, m)[0]; gb = gran_bridge(ac, m); base, tgt = gb or (ac, m)\\n      ops, _ = search(base, tgt, budget_s=10); medoid_depth = len(ops) if ops else 3; medoid_ops = ops or ['COMPOUND']\\n    coverage_status in {'ok', 'candidate_unparseable' (score 1.0), 'insufficient_pool'}\\n  diagnostics (track L ERROR items):\\n    medoid_correct = LABEL(m, ref) ∈ {CORRECT}\\n    blind_spot_strict = eqmv(c, m)                # the consensus endorses the error\\n    blind_spot_same_ops = (not medoid_correct) and ops(m→ref) == ops(c→ref)\\n    majority_endorses = eq_frac_full ≥ 0.5\\n    op agreement (depth-1/2 ERROR items, medoid_depth in {1, 2}):\\n      exact multiset match of medoid_ops vs labeller ops; any-overlap; coarse-class match\\n        (COVERAGE = ADD/DROP; POLARITY = NEG/REV/QUANT; STRUCT = the rest)\\n      chance baseline = the majority-class frequency\\n  SC scores: sc5_eq_frac_cheap = mean over the 5 samples' translation of t of eqmv(c, sample); missing/unparseable samples count as non-equivalent\\n    sc5_eq_frac_same likewise (gpt-3.5 candidates)\\n    sc5_entropy = semantic entropy of the 5 samples + c\\n  Time guard: log the per-candidate seconds; run mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> all.\\n    If the extrapolated total > 2 h, lower search budget_s to 5 for medoid_depth and skip the secondary peer-as-candidate repair search\\n    (keep eq_frac for them).\\n\\nSTEP 6. REWRITE INVARIANCE (≈20 min).\\n  select the 150 CORRECT items (both tracks) with the smallest item_id\\n  rewrites.py, deterministic, seed 0:\\n    RENAME: each predicate's head camelCase token -> the first WordNet synonym lemma from a different word in the first synset\\n      (CamelCased); if none, the token reversed + 'Q' suffix (a nonce); constants -> 'c_' + sha1(name)[:4]; arity preserved\\n    REORDER: swap the operands of every ∧/∨\\n    DEMORGAN: rewrite the first ∧ as ¬(¬A∨¬B) (or ∨ as ¬(¬A∧¬B))\\n    PRENEX: pull quantifiers to the front when the variables are distinct and the move is sound (only across ∧/∨, and across the consequent of →); else n/a\\n    CONTRAPOSITIVE: every A→B becomes ¬B→¬A\\n  assert: equivalent(rewrite, original) is True for all but RENAME; RENAME is equivalent after the inverse map\\n  score each rewritten candidate against the UNCHANGED pool with the same code\\n  report per family: FA rate = flagged (medoid_depth ≥ 1) among rewritten CORRECT items; flip rate vs the unrewritten version;\\n    mean |Δ eq_frac|\\n  Expect ≈ 0 for the z3-equivalent families BY CONSTRUCTION (say so). RENAME is the real test of the aligner.\\n\\nSTEP 7. ANALYSIS (analysis.py; ≈40 min).\\n  for each score in [c_score, medoid_depth, cluster_entropy, 1-eq_frac_peers6, 1-eq_frac_nonoai, 1-eq_frac_llm2, sc5_cheap, sc5_same]\\n  and each contrast (primary, lenient, track H):\\n    AUROC (sklearn), AUPRC with prevalence, 95% CI by sentence-clustered bootstrap (2000)\\n    tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Deutsch et al. 2023 acc_eq with the optimal tie epsilon; report both epsilon=0 and tie-calibrated)\\n    precision/recall/FA at the pre-registered threshold; precision re-weighted to 10% and 25% prevalence:\\n      prec_π = TPR·π / (TPR·π + FPR·(1-π))\\n  paired ΔAUROC with CIs: c_score - sc5_cheap; c_score - sc5_same (gpt-3.5 subset); c_score - cluster_entropy;\\n    full pool - peers6 pool\\n  cross-fitted logistic on [c_score, sc5_cheap] vs sc5_cheap alone (GroupKFold 5 by sentence) -> ΔAUROC CI\\n    Experiment D's disguised judge is NOT available here; iteration 2 joins it by item_id, so also emit oof-ready feature columns.\\n  per-system AUROC (gpt-3.5, gpt-4, davinci), each with n\\n  per-type sensitivity at threshold: by labeller op class (COVERAGE / POLARITY / STRUCT / mixed) and for UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND\\n    with Wilson CIs; the same on track H using the census classes\\n  POOL-SIZE CURVE: for k in {1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8}: 20 random pool subsets (seed 0..19), mean AUROC ± sd\\n  SHARED-ALIGNER CONFOUND:\\n    alignable_frac = the fraction of the candidate's predicates that are identical to, or align() to, a reference predicate\\n    AUROC on the VOCAB-CLEAN subset (alignable_frac == 1)\\n    partial Spearman of c_score with the label controlling for alignable_frac\\n    AUROC of alignable_frac alone (if ≥ c_score's, C is mostly the artefact: report that prominently)\\n  BLIND SPOTS: strict and same-ops rates with Wilson CIs, overall, per system, per len_bin, per op class\\n    medoid correctness vs the reference by stratum and by op class\\n    the ceiling this implies: max recall at medoid threshold = 1 - blind_spot_strict\\n  COMPLEXITY: AUROC and FA by len_bin, n_quant (≤1 / 2 / ≥3), depth, n_cond (0 / 1-2 / ≥3), exception y/n,\\n    each with (n_err, n_cor) and a 'testable' flag; If all fail, track L falls back to the 6 peers' own outputs as candidates (the secondary sample becomes primary) and this is flagged as a screen deviation in summary.json. The other experiments will have the same problem, so log it clearly. F2, match rate below 150 conclusions: apply the strategy's premise-extension rule only; do not add fuzzy matching. At $8.0 all optional arms stop; at $9.5 everything stops, and the partial results are analysed with that stated. log that the fallback condition fired)\\n    also try matching Logic-LM premise lines via the question/context text when the NL copy after ':::' is paraphrased\\n      use norm-equality only; NO fuzzy matching, to keep the screen identical across experiments\\n  TRACK H: all 302 curated items (202 FOLIO conclusions + 100 MALLS)\\n    candidate = original gold, ref = corrected gold, system = 'human_original'\\n  LABEL(cand, ref, ambiguous) -> one of\\n    UNPARSEABLE     (parse fails)\\n    CORRECT         (z3-equivalent exactly [auto_class EQUIV], OR equivalent after align() [auto_class VOCAB])\\n                     This matches the sibling Exp-A plan (EQUIV+VOCAB -> CORRECT, GRAN+COMPOUND -> UNCERTAIN), so label vectors join byte-identically.\\n                     Also emit the flag vocab_strict = every renamed predicate pair has identical toks() multisets;\\n                     a secondary analysis treats non-strict VOCAB as uncertain.\\n    UNCERTAIN_GRAN  (equivalent only after gran_bridge / reification)\\n    ERROR[ops]      (search(base, tgt, budget_s=25) finds depth ≤ 2; ops = sorted list)\\n    UNCERTAIN_COMPOUND (no repair)\\n    TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN (any z3 call returned None on the decisive step)\\n    track H only: if ambiguous == 'True' and not CORRECT -> READING_CHOICE (keep the underlying auto class in a field)\\n  STRATA, computed from the REFERENCE formula and the text, so they are identical across systems:\\n    words = len(text.split()); len_bin in {'<12','12-19','>=20'}\\n    n_quant = number of all/ex nodes\\n    depth = max AST nesting depth (every non-atom node adds 1)\\n    n_cond = sum over imp nodes of the number of top-level conjuncts in the antecedent\\n    exception = regex r'\\\\b(unless|except|excluding|other than|apart from|but not|without)\\\\b' on text.lower();
